The explicit unanswered-only continuation completed the five remaining candidate cells of the interrupted job in one successful native session and advanced to the next paired job. All ninety-five inherited answers and the unloaded failed startup remain preserved. The original campaign is still incomplete; the continuation has no complete quality result. Only execution counters, completion and file identities were inspected here, with no partial quality summary or answer-dependent selection.

Before any product answer, separate image and long-conversation protocols freeze both original and candidate arms, alternating order, native/artifact/Metal identities, source helpers, every case and its expected answer, complete native reply reservations and acceptance. Eight image conversations have twenty-four turns per arm; nine synthetic long conversations have twenty-seven. Images keep the explicit 14.5 GB process ceiling and 20.5 GB preflight. Text keeps fourteen and seventeen GB respectively. Both retain 32K context, two streamed original drafts, prefix reuse, seed seven and 512-token complete reply reservations. Every candidate turn must pass; these focused fixtures do not establish general image noninferiority or speed. Each family has six hours, at most two model sessions per case and 128 MB of receipts. The combined prospective receipts fit inside the unchanged total staging ceiling alongside the remaining general-task reservation. Execution is sequential after the active study.

The first copied product runtime failed read-only import before any model launch because flattening helpers lost the slotpack package directory. The preserved second freeze retains package paths and passes validation from a clean Python process using only its frozen helper directory. No case, answer, grade or resource limit was changed in this preparation correction. A serial coordinator is prepared; neither product family has run.

The metadata-only disk plan identifies ninety-six exact generated payload files: the losing affine refit and the redundant VQ 3.2 contiguous repack. Their planned file bytes total 100,714,474,672. Original checkpoints, the selected minmax candidate, frozen binaries, all source/progress/manifests and raw evidence remain retained. Exact original refit producer bytes are copied beside the plan, including the old transport writer recovered by digest from Git commit e9173521d104d69412883aceee715ba146729b23. The initial preparation refused the changed current writer before any deletion. The revised source recovery does not change the working checkout. The full VQ 3.2 source remains available for rebuilding its redundant repack.

The plan projects 418,572,658,578 staging bytes after retiring those allocated files, adding the complete 90,236,537,746-byte standalone export reservation and retaining the general-study receipt reservation. This is storage arithmetic from metadata, not a completed deletion or export. Future deletion requires exclusive native model/verification ownership, full payload authentication, unchanged regular-file identity, explicit paths and preserved metadata. No payload is deleted and no complete payload scan or export runs alongside the model study.

The official checkpoint license retrieved from [Qwen's published file](https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next/blob/de4b8e4d43b917e7706784d8bb445c9af86a3540/LICENSE) is byte-identical to the pinned license. Clause two describes a separate-license requirement for commercial use by the specified Model as a Service and AI Work Assistant businesses, with an internal-use exception. This engineering work does not establish whether Sevra falls within that clause or has any required permission. Product-use clearance remains a release input; implementation and local evaluation continue. No provider was contacted and no public model artifact or application release was published.

Raw completion metadata, prospective protocols, preparation failures, source recovery, storage arithmetic and license bytes follow. Home paths are normalized; original/normalized byte counts and hashes remain explicit. Compressed content is roundtrip checked. Partial model answers are not reproduced or summarized.
